ParlaMint-PL_2017-05-25-sejm-42-2.u609	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour to announce [[Game in the O.R., bell ringing.]] a statement on the 25th anniversary of family medicine in Poland and the World Family Doctor's Day. In 2012 I had the opportunity to make a statement on the 20th anniversary of family medicine in Poland. Round anniversaries I consider an excellent opportunity for some kind of summary, drawing conclusions and presenting a vision of development. Therefore, after the next 5 years I want to refer again to the condition and importance of Polish family medicine. In this case, I consider an in-depth reflection to be the duty and honor of every Member with a doctor's character in his hand, especially given the fact that I am a specialist in family medicine. I don't think there's anyone in this room who would object to a statement about the great, even superior role of family medicine in the public health system. In the United States of America, the importance of this branch of medicine was noted just after World War II, in Europe at the beginning of the 1970s. In our homeland we implemented good patterns from the West in fact only a few years after the fall of the Iron Curtain. Over the past quarter of a century, the health care system has been repeatedly reformed, not to say that it has given in to several revolutions. Currently, in Poland, a family doctor is considered to be a second-degree specialist in this field. Of course, it is an essential element of basic health care, but it is not the only one. Preventive, diagnostic, therapeutic, rehabilitation and care services are provided with the necessary assistance of an environmental and family nurse, an environmental and family midwife and a nurse in the teaching and education environment. The team, which together we create, plans and implements comprehensive care of the patient, including healthy Pole and his family. In the course of performing our duties, we pay more attention to the environment of the patient, his work, family, material situation, and even the characteristics of character and religion in a holistic manner than the doctor of any other specialization. Moreover, a family doctor is also the greatest guarantee of continuity of treatment - knowing the history of the patient's illness, disposition, habits, family history and lifestyle, he is able to coordinate the treatment process properly. By directing the patient to appropriate specialist clinics, hospital treatment, we have a great responsibility for an extended and reliable diagnosis of the health condition. This is not a simple task, because in breaks in a highly absorbing work we must constantly increase the resources of our knowledge and skills. The biggest pay for us are the positive effects of treatment, rehabilitation and increasing confidence in our patients. The potential of the family doctor, in addition to knowledge, acquired abilities, good relations with the patient, are also his personal qualities, as he should as a public person with a rich system of values, attitudes, opinions and emotions create his actions towards the patient. We must be able to find a balance between commitment, effort and our own vital forces, which are not limitless. Is the health care system in Poland conducive to the family doctor in the proper execution of the mission he takes on his shoulders? Is it not more worthwhile for a young doctor to choose a specialty than that which is the core of the health system, but which is unfortunately criticised in many environments? Has it developed since the last round anniversary of the 20th anniversary of family medicine in Poland, or on the contrary, because it came to function in more difficult and less predictable times? Does the State support the training process of family doctors? Are they coming or falling? Are they getting younger or older? The answers to these questions, which we know to a large extent, are unfortunately not good for us as a society. These are a number of questions which are rhetorical for people who know the condition of the Polish healthcare system. I dare also say that the discussion on family medicine in Poland is deprived of positive accents every year, and even worse, the actions of the current management of the health department prove that it unfortunately goes further than in many highly developed countries. At this point, I would like to thank all the founding fathers, [[♪ The bell ♪]] who 25 years ago undertook the effort to implement family medicine doctors in Poland, and all those who work in this profession for the benefit of the entire population of Poles. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-05-25-sejm-42-2.u615	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! This year, 25 years of uprising are celebrated by the State Fire Department, which is why I would like to bring up the story of the uprising of this much-neededed formation society. In the late '80s and early '90s. as a result of changes in the system, political and economic in Poland, as well as the growth of various kinds of threats, it became necessary to make radical changes in the fire protection system. As early as 1988, work was started on systemic legal and organisational solutions concerning a wide range of rescues in the form of new laws. They were preceded by problem analyses. Their creation was attended by representatives of fire marshals, fire schools, trade unions, as well as scientific communities. As a result of these actions, two drafts were drawn up and addressed to the Sejm: on fire protection and on the State Fire Guard. 1 July 1992 The PSP has been organized. At the same time, the implementation of adopted regulations and the implementation of numerous rescue tasks began. Another important task was the construction of a national rescue and fire-fighting system, the CRSG, which started functioning on 1 January 1995. In the following years, the system was expanded by including the most mobile units of voluntary firefighters. The reform of public administration entered into force on 1 January 1999. The implementation of the reform in the PSP required a great deal of work at all organisational levels. After almost 2 years of operation under new conditions, the State Fire Department and the national fire safety system have been strengthened. The public, expressing its opinion in subsequent surveys, sees the State Fire Department as the most mobile and best prepared rescue formation. The State Fire Department has a very lively activity in the international forum. International rescue cooperation agreements have been signed with most neighbouring countries in Poland. 25 years of the State Fire Service is a time of intense technological and technological change that is a source of new, previously unknown threats. During this time, firefighters have become a professional formation of qualified people, prepared substantively and physically to take action towards a single goal, i.e. to ensure optimal fire safety throughout the country. I thank all the firefighters for their daily hard work, help and rescue, which they carry with them at risk of their lives. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]
ParlaMint-PL_2017-05-25-sejm-42-2.u616	Thank you. Mr Jarosław Sachajko. You're welcome.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-05-25-sejm-42-2.u617	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Ladies and gentlemen! I'd like to make a statement on the central celebration of the Border Guard. On 16 May 2017, the Grand Market in Zamość hosted ceremonies related to the central celebrations of the Border Guard. This event was a big celebration for border guards, residents of the region and the local community. The ceremony was honored by the President of the Republic of Poland Andrzej Duda, as well as representatives of government administration, local government and parliamentarians. In view of the above, I would like to express my deep regret at the absence of an invitation to such an extraordinary ceremony, although last year I expressed my wish in writing to attend the ceremonies organized by the Border Guard. Before the ceremony began, I tried to receive the appropriate invitation. Unfortunately, my efforts were futile. Being convinced that the celebrations are addressed to an open group of Members, I decided to take part in them, but I was not allowed in. The results also did not result in identification as a Member of the Republic of Poland. From the Deputy Director - Deputy Chief of Staff of the Chief Border Guard of Mr Mariusz Telepka, I heard that the guest list was consulted with Minister Mariusz Błaszczak and I cannot be allowed in. In my view, discrimination against Members of the opposition causes unnecessary escalation of conflicts and antagonisation of society. I would like to express my hope that similar situations will not take place in the future and that representatives of all political groups will receive invitations. At the same time, on the occasion, I would like to present to all Border Guard officers and their families and close wishes for all prosperity, strength and health to continue to work and work in the service of the Republic of Poland. The second part of the statement concerns a vote, in fact, a situation which took place before the vote on the Law on the legal protection of plant varieties. Mr Robert Telus accused me of caring for the interests of farming companies, not farmers. Here I would like to tell the High House and all of you that Mr Telus does not understand the very close relationship between breeding companies, Polish breeding companies and farmers. If we lead Polish breeding companies to disappear from the market, they will not be there, then farmers automatically lose good Polish varieties, they lose their ability to buy varieties at good market prices. They will be condemned to dictation of foreign companies, dictation of foreign seed companies, which will not have varieties adapted to our soil and climate conditions, which will cause problems with dreaming, drought problems, problems with optimal yield. At the same time, we have already sold SHiUZs, i.e. animal insemination companies, the largest company in Poland, the French. Now it is not strange that French farmers will not care about good breeds of Polish animals at the moment, because there is direct competition with Polish farmers. I wouldn't want the same thing happening to seed companies. Therefore, I believe that the adopted law does nothing, it is a law only to show farmers that it is supposed to care about them, without looking forward to the existence of Polish seed companies that will care about the Polish farmer. Thank you. [[Applause]]
ParlaMint-PL_2017-05-25-sejm-42-2.u618	Thank you very much, sir. That concludes our parliamentary statements. Information about the impact of the interpellation, queries and answers to them is available in the Sejm Information System. This concludes the 42nd session of the Sejm of the Republic of Poland. The minutes of the sitting will be submitted to the Secretariat of the Sejm. The agenda for the 43rd session of the Sejm, appointed on 7, 8 and 9 June 2017, will be delivered to the ladies and gentlemen. I wish you parliamentarians a pleasant weekend while still present. Enjoy your rest, too, for your current staff. We're adjourned. [[The Vice Marshal strikes three times with a marshal's cane]] [[The meeting is adjourned at 3 p.m. 22 min 07]]
